Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any sheaves module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 387. The projective resolution has length at most 665 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on sheaves structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 387. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 665. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in sheaves theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 845 and the invariant I(A,B) = 834 mod 2.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to sheaves theory, we decompose the problem into 845 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 834. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
